                                - text: for this builds set up.
                              - paragraph [ref=e863]:
                                - text: When choosing your

                                  - text: ":"
                                  - strong [ref=e875]: Mace

                          - generic [ref=e1083]:
                            - heading "Build Overview" [level=3] [ref=e1084]
                            - heading "Arsenal Selection" [level=3] [ref=e1085]
                            - paragraph [ref=e1086]:
                              - text: This build will be running on a
                              - strong [ref=e1087]: Dual Wield
                              - text: set up in order to take advantage of
                              - generic [ref=e1092]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                              - text: combined with
                              - generic [ref=e1097]: Shard of Verathiel
                              - text: .
                            - list [ref=e1098]:
                              - listitem [ref=e1099]:
                                - strong [ref=e1100]: "If you would like to learn how to set your weapon selection watch this video:"
                                - link "How to Select Weapons video" [ref=e1101] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ?si=Lz-LY5MK3EehP_CC
                            - heading "Fury Management" [level=3] [ref=e1102]
                            - paragraph [ref=e1103]:
                              - generic [ref=e1108]: Shard of Verathiel
                              - text: will cause
                              - generic [ref=e1113]: Frenzy
                              - text: to cost
                              - strong [ref=e1114]: Fury
                              - text: . That combined with
                              - generic [ref=e1119]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                              - text: can be a strain on your fury. Below is your steps to manage that.
                            - heading "Affixes" [level=4] [ref=e1120]
                            - list [ref=e1121]:
                              - listitem [ref=e1122]:
                                - strong [ref=e1123]: Resource Generation
                                - text: will help increase the amount of
                                - strong [ref=e1124]: Fury
                                - emphasis [ref=e1125]
                                - text: you gain and pairs really well with
                                - generic [ref=e1130]: Rallying Cry
                                - text: . You can
                                - strong [ref=e1131]: Temper
                                - text: this onto your
                                - emphasis [ref=e1132]: Rings
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=e1133]: Amulet.
                              - listitem [ref=e1134]:
                                - strong [ref=e1135]: Fury Regeneration
                                - text: needs to be added onto the Helmet, Chest piece, Pants and Boots.
                              - listitem [ref=e1136]:
                                - text: Do not spam
                                - generic [ref=e1141]: Frenzy
                                - text: if you are not near monsters if you can't maintain
                                - strong [ref=e1142]: Fury
                                - text: .
                            - heading "Defense (Non-Selig)" [level=4] [ref=e1143]
                            - list [ref=e1144]:
                              - listitem [ref=e1145]:
                                - text: Take all defense nodes inside the
                                - emphasis [ref=e1146]: Paragon
                                - text: board first. All
                                - emphasis [ref=e1147]: Maximum Life
                                - text: ","
                                - emphasis [ref=e1148]: Resistances,
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=e1149]: Armor
                                - text: nodes.
                              - listitem [ref=e1150]:
                                - text: You can use glyphs like
                                - generic [ref=e1155]: Ire
                                - text: or
                                - generic [ref=e1160]: Territorial
                                - text: to help keep yourself alive.
                              - listitem [ref=e1161]:
                                - text: Make sure all your gear has
                                - emphasis [ref=e1162]: Maximum Life
                                - text: on it and you can then add
                                - emphasis [ref=e1163]: Resistances,
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=e1164]: Armor
                                - text: as needed.
                            - paragraph [ref=e1165]:
                              - strong [ref=e1166]: "If you want to learn how to set your defenses up, watch this video:"
                              - link "How to avoid getting one-shot video" [ref=e1167] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/5IrPJ-H_uLI?si=yDaUSCn8owx0mDWj
                            - heading "Offense" [level=4] [ref=e1168]
                            - paragraph [ref=e1169]:

                              - text: .
